HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B005","root_000217/B009","root_001507/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_12bd158becaf3f98eb4f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:19:2\",\"root_000217\",[\"B005\",\"B009\"]],[\"88:19:4\",\"root_001507\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sertlik ve engelleme çağrışımları, dağı düz yüzeydeki bir kusur değil, sınırsız düzleşmeyi ve geçişi keserek araziye yapı kazandıran yerleştirilmiş direnç yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_15d4c694b6531f8eec74","text":"1. **Mountains mediate between raised sky and leveled earth as fixed vertical masses.** **Lexical:** `ج ب ل` B001, \"high solid mass,\" and `ن ص ب` B001, \"setting something upright and prominent.\" **Cue:** the mountain line is placed between sky raised in 88:18 and earth surfaced in 88:20, all under repeated `وَإِلَى ... كَيْفَ`. **Role:** mountain solidity supplies mass; setting upright supplies position; the two neighboring ayat supply upper and lower reference planes. **Abduction:** I infer that mountains structurally mediate those planes rather than merely occur between their descriptions. **Reading change:** the mountains become vertical joints in a three-part world arrangement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1b5940e99b01ef584361","text":"4. **The social image of mountain-like chiefs is corrected by the later authority boundary.** **Lexical:** `ج ب ل` B012, \"chiefs like mountains,\" activated by the mass image. **Cue:** after the mountain, 88:21 assigns reminder, 88:22 denies the messenger controlling authority, and 88:24 names God as punishing agent. **Role:** B012 opens a human authority analogy; the later sequence limits and reallocates it. **Abduction:** I infer that mountain-scale authority would be an attractive but rejected transfer to the reminder. **Reading change:** the ayah can evoke stable leadership while the window sharply prevents that image from turning the human messenger into a dominating mountain over others.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B001","root_001507/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1cb05b35bd560b701119","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:19:2\",\"root_000217\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:19:4\",\"root_001507\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dağlar, yükseltilmiş gök ile yayılmış yer arasında duran sağlam dikey eklemler olarak üç parçalı mekânsal düzeni birbirine bağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B001","root_001507/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2deb40cc5a77ac182958","text":"1. **Mountains as upright, prominent installations.** Evidence: `ج ب ل` B001 high solid mass; `ن ص ب` B001 setting upright and prominent. `ك ي ف` has no branch inventory. Structural cue: sky raised, mountains set, earth spread. Role: mountains are the vertical fixtures between upper sky and lower earth. Reading change: the ayah reads as attention to emplacement, not only mountain existence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B004","root_001507/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2f9cbdf9a568a4417aed","text":"2. **Innate formed nature fixed visibly.** Evidence: `ج ب ل` B004 innate formed nature; `خ ل ق` B001/B003 creation/proportion from 88:17; `ن ص ب` B006 fixed base/origin/threshold. Abductive move: nature is visible as fixed proportion and stable base. Reading change: the mountains become signs of created disposition and fixity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B005","root_000217/B009","root_001507/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3e0ea5cdb84a54907ea1","text":"3. **Mountain hardness productively interrupts an otherwise extended surface.** **Lexical:** `ج ب ل` B005, \"hardness that stops digging,\" and B009, difficulty or prevention; `ن ص ب` B001 fixes that hardness upright. **Cue:** the next ayah describes the earth as spread and level, so the mountain stands as a deliberate break in continuity. **Role:** hardness blocks unlimited penetration or flattening, preserving differentiated terrain. **Abduction:** I infer a functional brake from the adjacency of mountain and surface; no geological process is supplied. **Reading change:** the mountain is not a defect in level earth but an installed resistance that gives the surface structure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000217/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4b8ef6804d6e33971d58","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:19:2\",\"root_000217\",[\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Dağ through distinct spatial objects, and the mountain is the most locally prominent form in that route. **Role:** the marker branch gives solid elevation an orienting function within the observer's field. **Abduction:** I infer wayfinding and boundary from prominence; the ayah does not name travel or borders. **Reading change:** `نُصِبَتْ` presents mountains not only as erected matter but as the landscape's readable coordinates.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8a3cc8330b0b1daf1839","text":"The
